patippurai
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimaiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷaiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolainōkkup pārvaiyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉaivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉaittaiyum cērttu avaruṭaiya maṟaivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭuraikaḷai ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nilai aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉaivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭuraip putaiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇaiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷai naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉaivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟaiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamaiyuṭaivar eṉṟu maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭaikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟaiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñarai iṉṉum peṟṟut taravillai eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturaiyarum, kuṟaimatiyar tēkkivaitta karaiyiruḷai nīkka vanta maṟaimalaiyār vaḻivanta niṟaimalaiyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravaittalaivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉaivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumaikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷai mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumai koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉaiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam uraippaṉa. tamiḻai āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimaippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyai mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟai ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamaiyākum.
pāvāṇarait tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkaikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilaittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷaiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talaippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷai veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟaikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟaiyum, citaikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyaiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putaiyalkaḷai tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyai mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷai vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamaiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷai ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉaivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tantai periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷkai
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkaikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷaip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.




vāṉmaḻai vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyiraip paṇayam vaittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷai eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷai iṭaiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vaippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇai
yākavum ivai iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vaippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avai cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟaik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟamainta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷai īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻaipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟait tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakaiya pēṇutal uṭaiyavai eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavai tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kaippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭamai kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varaivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamaiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamaikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakaiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vaitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭaikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamaiyallavō!
akkaṭamaiyai avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇaiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvai! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭai puritalukku mēṟkoṭai illaiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavai pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭurai poḻivu mutalām vakaiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varaivu pā varaivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟai ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻai vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭamai puriyum vīṟuṭaiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakaiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭaiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭaippukaḷai mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vaikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippai vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.

tamiḻ vaḷam








1
vērccoṟ cuvaṭi
1. ar
	ar = olik kuṟippu.
(a)	aravam = oli.
	arā, aravu, aravam = iraivatu, oliyaṟivatu, pāmpu
(b)		ari = ar eṉṉum oli tōṉṟac ciṟitu ciṟitāyk kaṭi 		allatu tiṉ.
	arāvu = tēy. aram = arāvuvatu. arampam = arāvuvatu, 		aṟuppatu.
	ari = aṟu. kōṭu (kiḷai) + ari = kōṭari - kōṭāri -kōṭāli.
	arivāḷ = arikiṉṟa vāḷ. arivāḷmaṇai.
	ari = aḻi. ari = aḻippatu, pakaivaṉ, ciṅkam.
	kōḷ + ari = kōḷari.
	araṅku = aṟutta aṟai, icai nāṭaka mēṭai.
	araṅkam = āṟṟiṭai aṟukkappaṭṭa nilam.
	tiruvaraṅkam - srīraṅkam.
	arakku = tēy, aḻi.
	arakkaṉ = irāṭcataṉ.
	araṉ = aḻippavaṉ, tēvaṉ, civaṉ.
	aracu = pakaivarai aḻippavaṉ, vēntaṉ. aracu + aṉ = aracaṉ.
	aracu = talaimaiyāṉa allatu teyvam taṅkum maram.
	aracaṉ - araicaṉ - araiyaṉ - rāyaṉ - Roy
2. aṟu
	ar - aṟu.
	aṟu = veṭṭu, piḷa, piri, nīṅku, nīkku.
	aṟuvāḷ = aṟukkiṉṟa vāḷ.
	aṟavu = nīkkam, varaiyaṟu - varaiyaṟavu.
	aṟuti = muṭivu.
	aṟai = aṭi, aṟukkappaṭṭa allatu pirikkappaṭṭa iṭam. 
	uṟuppaṟai = uṟuppaṟukkappaṭṭatu. varaiyaṟu - varaiyaṟai.
	aṟuku = aṟuttaṟut tōṭip paṭarum pul.
	aṟuvai = aṟukkum tuṇi.
	aṟuppu = payirai allatu tāliyai aṟuttal.
	aṟuvaṭai = aṟuppu.
	aṟṟam = piṟar illāta camaiyam.
3. ar
	ar - aru = aḻi, kuṟai, neruṅku.
	aruku = kuṟaivāku, neruṅku, pakkam.
	arukaṇmai - arukāmai.
	arukaṉ = neruṅkiṉavaṉ, urimaiyuṭaiyavaṉ
	arukatai = urimai.
	arumai = kuṟaivu, kūṭāmai, ciṟappu, viruppam.
	arumaivanta - arumanta.
	aruntal = kuṟaivu, vilai yuyarvu.
	aritu = kūṭātatu, varuttamāṉatu.
4. aḷ
	ar - aḷ - aṇ - aṭu.
(a)	aḷ = neruṅku, neruṅkac cērtteṭu.
	aṇ, aṇṇu = neruṅku.
	aṇmu = neruṅku. 
	aṇmai, aṇumai, aṇimai = camīpam.
	aṇuku = neruṅku.
	aṇṭai = pakkam. 7ām vēṟṟumai urupu
	aṭu = neruṅku, aṭar = neruṅku. aṭavi = neruṅkiṉa kāṭu,
	aṭukku = neruṅkavai.
	aṭutta = neruṅkiṉa, pakkamāṉa. iṉṉoru.
(b)	aṭai = aṭuttatu, appam. aṭai - āṭai.
	pālaṭai = pālāṭai, caṅku.
	aṭukkum = neruṅkum, takum. 'uṉakkitu aṭukkumā?.'
aṭaṅ° =neruṅku,uḷḷamai.
	aṭakku (piṟa viṉai).
	aṭakkam = oṭukkam, uḷḷīṭu, putaippu.
	aṭai = neruṅku, cēr, peṟu.
	(ṭa = ya) ayal - acal = pakkam, aṉṉiyam
5. ar
	ar = civappu a - i. r - l
	Ürattam= ªcannīr,Ülattakam= ªcampañcuk°ḻampu.	arakku = civanta meḻuku.
	aruṇaṉ = civappu. 
	aruṇam = kālaic cūriyaṉ.
	ariṇam = civappu, māṉ. 
	irattam = cennīr.
	iratti, ilantai = civanta paḻattaiyuṭaiya muṭceṭi.
	irāki = kēḻvaraku.
6. i
	i - kīḻuṟaṟ kuṟippu.
	iṟaṅku = kīḻē vā.
	iru = kīḻ uṭkār, taṅku, vācañcey.
	kuṭiyiru = vācañcey.
	iruppu = taṅkal, rokkam.
	irukkai = ācaṉam.
	iḻi = iṟaṅku, kīḻāku.
	iḷi = iḻivu.
7. kil
	i - kil.
(a)	kil - kal = tōṇṭu, tōṇṭuṅ karuvi.
	kil - kiḷ - kīḷ - kīḻ.
(b)	kiḷ, kiḷḷu = nakattaik kīḻē pati, kiḷḷukkīrai.
	kiḷḷai, kiḷḷi - kiḷi = kaṉikaḷaik kiḷḷuvatu.
(c)	kiḻi = kīṟu, tuṇiyaik kiḻi.
	kiḻi = muṭiccu, tuṇi, paṭam.
	kīḷ = kiḻi. 'kīḷār kōvaṇam'.
(d)	kīḻ. kīḻkku - kiḻakku = kīḻiṭam, oru ticai.
(e)	kīṟu = kōṭu kiḻi. kīṟṟu = kīṟi aṟutta tuṇṭu, tuṇṭu.
	kiṟukku = kōṭu kīṟu, paittiyam.
	kīṟal = kōṭiḻaittal, eḻuttaṟiyāmai uṇarttuñ col.
	kīccu = kīṟu.
8. i
	i = piṉṉiṭaṟ kuṟippu.
(a)	iṭaṟu = piṉviḻat taṭukku.
	iṭai = piṉṉiṭu, tōṟṟōṭu. iṭakkai = tōṟṟa kai.
	iṭam = tōlvi, iṭar = tuṉpam.
	o.nā : valakkai = veṟṟi peṟṟa allatu valiya kai.
	iṭaiñcal = piṟ celuttum taṭai.
(b)	iṭa = iḻuttup paṟi.
	iṇuku, iṇuṅku = iḻuttup paṟi.
	iḻu = piṉṉukkuk koṇṭu vā.
	iṟai = taṇṇīrai iḻu, piṉṉukkut teṟi, teṟi.
9. iḻu
	i - iḷ - iḻu = piṉṉukkuk koṇṭuvā.
	iḻu - iḻuku = piṉṉōkkit taṭavu.
(a)	iḻuppu = iḻuttal. iḻuvai = iḻuttuk kaṭattal.
	iḻutu = iḻukum mai.
	iḻutu - eḻutu = iḻuttu varai.
	ilakku, ilakki = eḻutu. ilakku = eḻuttu.
	ilakku + iyam = ilakkiyam = nūṟṟokuti.
	ilakku + aṇam = ilakkaṇam = moḻiyoḻuṅku.
	o.nā : E. literature from letter; grammer from Gk. grammos, letter
	ilakkam = eḻuttu, eṇkuṟi, eṇ.
(b)	iḻu - īr = iḻu, iḻuttaṟu.
	iḻu - icu - ici. iḻuppu = icivu = jaṉṉi.
	iḻukku = iḻukuvatu, vaḻukkal, vaḻu.
	iḷai = mūcciḻu.
10. iḷ
	i - iḷ.
(a)	iḷai = mūcciḻu, meli.
	iḷaippu = mūcciḻuppu, melivu, taḷarcci.
	iḷaippu + āṟu = iḷaippāṟu.
	īḷai = kōḻai, iḷaippu.
	iḻai = iḻuttuc cetukku, nūliḻu.
	iḻaippu + uḷi = iḻaippuḷi.
	iḻai = iḻaittuc ceyta nakai, iḻutta nūl.
	iḻaippu = kāca nōy.
	iḷaittavaṉ = melintavaṉ, ciṟiyavaṉ.
	'ūruk kiḷaittavaṉ piḷḷaiyār kōyil āṇṭi'
(b)	iḷam, iḷamai = ciṟu paruvam.
	iḷaval = tampi. iḷaiñaṉ = vālipaṉ, vīraṉ.
	iḷaiyavaṉ = āṇṭiṟ ciṟiyavaṉ.
(c)	iḷappam = tāḻvu, iḷakkāram = meṉmaipaṟṟi mēṟcellal.
	iḷaku = mellitāku, iḷakkam = meṉmai.
	iṇaṅku = meṉmaiyāku, uṭaṉpaṭu.
	ilaku = eḷitu, kaṉamiṉmai. ilēcu = eḷitu, kaṉamiṉmai.
	ikaḻ = eḷimaiyā yeṇṇu.

12. piṉ
	i - piṉ

	piṉṟu = piṉ.
	pintu = piṟpaṭu.
(b)	piṉ - piṟa, piṟam, piṟampu, piṟaku, piṟakku, piṟakkiṭu.

(c)	piṟa = maṟṟa. piṟaṉ = maṟṟavaṉ, ayalāṉ, piṟattiyāṉ = ayalāṉ.
13. puṟa
	piṟa - puṟa - puṟam - puṟaṉ.
	puṟam = piṉ, mutuku, veḷi, veḷippakkam, mēṟpakkam, 			pakkam.

	puṟappaṭu = vīṭṭaiviṭṭu veḷippaṭu, payaṇan toṭaṅku.

	puṟaṅkāṭṭu, puṟaṅkoṭu = mutuku kāṭṭu, tōṟṟōṭu.
	puṟam = puṟapporuḷ, puṟam.
	puṟaṉ = puṟaṅkūṟṟu.
14. iṭu.

17. iṟu

21. muṉ.

	mutuku = piṉpuṟam. mūttōr = āṇṭil mikuntōr, periyōr.

	mutiyōr = kiḻavar, mūttōr. mutumakaṉ = kiḻavaṉ.

40. mē.

	mēlukku = mēṟpārvaikku, veḷikku.
	mēṉi = mēṟpuṟam, uṭampu.
	mēṭu = uyaramāṉa iṭam.
	mēṭu - mōṭu. mēṭai = mēṭāṉa iṭam.
(b)	miku = mēlāku, miñcu.
	micai = mēl.
	miñcu = mikutiyāku, aḷavu kaṭa. miñcu - viñcu = miku.
	miccam = mīti.
(c)	mī = mēl, mītiyāku.
	mīmicai = mika mēl, ōr ilakkaṇam.
	mīti = mikuti. mīttam = mīttu vaitta poruḷ. mītam = 		mīti.
	mītu = mēl, 7ām vēṟṟumai urupu.
(d)	mīṟu = varampu kaṭa.
	mīṟu - vīṟu = perumai, perumai koḷ.
	vīṟṟu + iru = vīṟṟiru = perumaiyuṭaṉiru.
(e)	mettu = mēṟkoḷ, tōṟkaṭi. mettai = mēlvīṭu.
	meccu = uyarttip pēcu.
41. ai.
	ai = viyappu, perumai.
	ai viyappākum' (tol. uri. 89)
	aiyaṉ = periyōṉ, talaivaṉ, tantai, aracaṉ, kaṭavuḷ, āciriyaṉ, muṉivaṉ, civaṉ.
	aiyaṉār = cāttaṉār.
	tam + aiyaṉ = tamaiyaṉ = aṇṇaṉ.
	aiyai = ammai, pārvati. 
	aiyā = aiyaṉ eṉpataṉ viḷi.
	aiyō = (aiyaṉ eṉpataṉ viḷi) irakkakkuṟippu.
42. ō.
	ō = olikkuṟippu.

(b)	ol = olikkuṟippu. ol - kol = olikkuṟippu.
	ol - oli. olleṉa = viraivāka.
43. ō.
	ō = uyarak kuṟippu.

(b)	ō - ōm - ōmpu = uyaramākku, vaḷar, pātukā.

	ō - o
	oyyāram = uyaram, uyarvu.

44. u.
	ō - u = uyarakkuṟippu.
	uka = uyar
	uccam = uyarnilai.

	uvā = yāṉai.

45. ō.
	ō = poruntu.
(a)	ō + iyam = ōviyam = oppaṉai, cittiram.
	ō - o. o = poruntu.
	oṭṭu = poruntu, oru tuṇaiviṉai.
	ceyya + oṭṭār = ceyya voṭṭār = ceyyaviṭār.
	oṭṭumā = poruttu māmaram.
	oṭṭu = piciṉ, cūḷ.

	ottu = mēl vaitteṭu, taṭṭu.

	oṟṟar = oṟṟupavar.
46 u.

48. kaḷ
	kaḷ = karuppu.
(a)	kaḷḷam = karuppu, maṟaivu.
	kaḷḷaṉ, kaḷvaṉ = maṟaivāyk koḷpavaṉ.
	kaḷavu = maṟaivu, tiruṭu.
	kaḷā, kaḷavu = oru karuppup paḻam, paḻukkum maram.
	kaḷi = karuppu maṇ, kaḷi pōṉṟa uṇavu.
	kaḷ = pulaṉai maṟaikkum matu. kaḷi = kaṭkuṭiyaṉ, makiḻ.
	kaḷḷaṉ - kaṇṇaṉ = karuppaṉ.
(b)	kāḷam = karuppu.
	kāḷi = karuppāṉa pēyt talaivi.
	am + kāḷam (kāḷi) + ammai = aṅkāḷammai.
	kāḷāṉ = karuṅkāḷāṉ.
	kāḷai = kariya erutu, erutu.
(c)	kaḷ - kara = maṟai.
	karavu = kapaṭam, kaḷavu.
49. kaṇ.
	kaḷ - kaṇ. = karuppāṉa viḻi.
(a)	kaṇ = viḻi, kaṇ pōṉṟatu, 7ām vēṟṟumai urupu
	kaṇṇu = maṉatāl pār, karutu.
	kaṇṇiyam = matippu.
	kaṇvāy = ciṟu vāykkāl.
	kaṇṇāḷaṉ, kaṇṇavaṉ, kaṇavaṉ = maṉaivikkuk kaṇ 		pōṉṟavaṉ.
	kaṇṇi = kaṇ kaṇṇāyk kaṭṭiya mālai.
	kaṇu = kaṇ pōṉṟa varaiyiṭam.
(b)	kaṇi = aḷaviṭu. kaṇiyaṉ = kaṇippavaṉ, cōtiṭaṉ.
	kaṇitam = kaṇakku. kaṇakku + aṉ = kaṇakkaṉ, kaṇakkap piḷḷai.
	kaṇakku = varavu celavuk kuṟippu.
	kaṇi - kuṇi = aḷaviṭu.
(c)	kaṇ - kāṇ = pār. kāṭci = aṟivu.
	kāṇi = mēṟpār, mēṟpārkkum nilam, pirivu.
	kāṇam = mēṟpārvai, mēṟpārvai nilam, pirivu.
	kaṇkāṇam = mēṟpārvai. kaṇkāṇi = mēṟpārppavaṉ.
	kaṇkāṇiyār = attiyaṭcar.
	mā + kāṇi = mākāṇi, mā + kāṇam = mākāṇam.
50. karu.
	kaḷ - karu = karuppāku.
(a)	karuppu = karumai, pañcam, pēy.
	karuku = karuppāku, tīntupō.
	karukku = kaṣāyam, paṉaimaṭṭaiyiṉ kariya ōram, kūr.
	karukal = poruḷ viḷaṅkāmai.
	karumpu = kariya taṇṭuḷḷa taṭṭai.
	karunāṭakam - karunaṭam - kaṉṉaṭam = oru nāṭu, oru moḻi.
(b)	karampu = karumaṇ. karampai = kāynta kaḷimaṇ, oru payaṟu.
(c)	kari = aṭuppuk kari, yāṉai.
	karical = kariya nilam,
	kariccāṉ = karikkuruvi. kāri = kariyatu.
	kariyaṉ = tirumāl, kaṇṇaṉ.
(d)	karu - kaṟu.
	kaṟu = karuppāku, mukaṅkaru, kōpi.
	kaṟuvu = kōpam, varmam, kaṟam = varmam.
51. kala.
	kala = kūṭu.
(a)	kalappu = cērppu, kalappaṭam = iḻikalavai.

	kalampakam - kalampam = kalavai.

(b)	kalakam = caṇṭai. kalāpam = kalakam, caṇṭai.
	kalām = caṇṭai.
(c)	kalaṅku = pala poruḷ kūṭu, mayaṅku.
	kalakkam = mayakkam.
52. kuḷ.
	kuḷḷam, kuḷḷal, kuḷḷai = kuṟumai.
(a)	kuñcu, kuñci = paṟavaip piḷḷai.
	kuccu, kucci = ciṟu kampu.
	kuṭṭi = ciṟiyatu, piḷḷai. kuruḷai = kuṭṭi.
	kuṭṭai, kuṇṭu = ciṟu kuḷam; kuḻi = ciṟu vaḷai.
	kuṭṭai - kaṭṭai = kuṟukiyatu.
	kukkal = kuḷḷanāy.
	kuṉṟu = kuṟai, ciṟumalai. kuṉṟi = ciṟu muttu.
	kuṉṟu + am = kuṉṟam.
(b)	kuṟu - kuṟaḷ = kuṟu māṉuṭam, kuṟu veṇpā.
	kuṟaḷi = kuṟum pēy.
	kuṟuku = ciṟu, kuṭṭaiyāku.
	kuṟukku = kuṟukiya vaḻi, ūṭu, naṭumutuku.
	kuṟai = taṉmai kuṟaintatu. kuṟṟam = kuṇak kuṟaivu.
	kuṟṟi - kucci = turumpu. kuṟu + il = kuṟṟil - kuccil
	kuṟumpu = kuṟumalai, kuṟumalaiyaracaṉ, avaṉceyyum 			cēṭṭai, cēṭṭai.
	kuṟumakaṉ - kuṟumāṉ = ciṟuvaṉ.
	kūḻal = kuṟukiyatu.
	kūḻi = kuṟumpacu.
	kūḻai = kuṟukiya muṭi.
53. kum
	kum = kuvi, tiraḷ.
	kum + al = kummal. kummi = kai kuvittāṭum āṭṭam
	kum + ar = kumar. kumar + i = kumari.
	kumar + aṉ = kumaraṉ. kumaraṉ - kumāraṉ.
	kumiḻ, kumiḻi = kuvitta nurai, kuvinta kaṭṭi.
	kumiḻ - cimiḻ - timil.
	kumi + al = kumiyal, kumi - kuvi.
	kuvi - kuviyal, kuvāl, kuvavu.
	kuvai = kuviyal, tiraṭci. kuvai - kukai.
	kuppu - kuppal = kuviyal.
	kuppuṟu = kuvi, talaikīḻāku.
	kuppi = kuvinta mūṭi. kuppai = kuviyal, tūcikkuviyal.
	kuppam = kuppaikkāṭṭu ūr.
	kuppaṉ = paṭṭik kāṭṭāṉ.
	kumpu = kuvi, kūṭu, kuviya vēku. kumpal = kūṭṭam.
	kumpi = kuvinta vayiṟu.
	kumpiṭu = kaikuvi.
	kumpam = kuvinta kuṭam. kumpā = kuvinta pāttiram.
	kūmpu = kuvi, pāymaram. kūppu = kai kuvi.
	kommai = tiraṭci.
54. koḷ.
	koḷ = vāṅku, peṟu, piṭi, mikutiyāyeṭu.

	koḷḷai = cūṟai, vilai.

	koḷu = poruṭ kuṟippu.
	koḷuvu = poruttu, māṭṭu.
	koḷuttu = paṟṟavai, poruttu, pukaṭṭu
	koḷai = paṇṇamaittal
	koḷ - kōḷ = koḷḷutal, kollutal, piṭittal, 				  peṟutal, karuttu, koḷkai, tītu collal.

55. civa
	cival = cennilam. civalai = ceṅkāḷai.
(a)	civam = civappu, tīkkaṭavuḷ, civaṉ.

	civam - civaṉ, civai = umai
	civa - tuva - tuvar = civappu, kācukkaṭṭi, ataṉ cuvai.
		tuvarai = civanta payaṟu.
(b)	civa - cem.
	cemmai = cenniṟam, oḻuṅku, nērmai.
	cekkar = cevvāṉam. cemmāṉ = cakkiliyaṉ.
	cempu = civanta ulōkam, ataṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa nīrppāttiram.
	cemmal = nērmaiyuḷḷavaṉ, talaivaṉ.
	ceppam = cīrāṉa nilai.
	cevvai = ceppam, cīr, cevvi = takunta camaiyam.
	cevvaṉ = cevvai.
(c)	civa - cē.
	cēntaṉ = civantavaṉ, murukaṉ.
	cēy = civantavaṉ, kuḻantai, murukaṉ
	cēyōṉ = murukaṉ.
56. cuḷ

57. cur.

	cūr = accam. cūraṉ = vīraṉ.
58. curi

59. cul
	cur - cul = cūṭṭāl vaḷai

60. curu.

61. cuḷi.
	cuḷ - cuḷi = cūṭṭāl mukam vaḷai.

62. curuḷ

	curuḷ = ōlaic curuḷ. curuṭṭai = vaḷainta muṭi.

	uruṭai = rōtai. uruṇṭai - uṇṭai.
63. cuṭu.

	cuṭalaiyāṭi = civaṉ.
64. teḷ
	teḷ - teḷḷu = māvait teḷivākku, tūymaiyākku.

	teri = teḷivāka aṟi, terintukoḷ.
	tēr = teḷivupeṟu, tiṟampeṟu, ārāy.

	tēṟal = teḷivu, tēṉ.
	tēṟṟam = teḷivu, uṟuti.
	tēṟṟāṅ koṭṭai = nīrait teḷivākkum oru koṭṭai.
	tēṉ = teḷintatu.
	tēṉ - tī - tīvu, titti, teviṭṭu
65. tēy.
(a)	tēy - tēyu = tēyntuṇṭākum neruppu.
	tēyvu - tēvu, tēvaṉ, tēvatai = nerupput taṉmaiyuḷḷa 		teyvam, kaṭavuḷ.
	tēy - teyvu - teyvam.
	tēvu - tē.
(b)	tēy - tī = neruppu. tī - tīmai, tīṅku.
	tīmpu = tīyiṉ taṉmai, pollāṅku.
	tīvam - tīpam = viḷakku.
	tī - tīy = cuṇṭu, kāy, vāṭu.
66. paku
	paku = vaku.
(a)	paku + al = pakal = naṭu, naṭuppakal, pakalvēḷai, pirivu.
	pakalōṉ = cūriyaṉ. pakal - pāl = pirivu.
	paku + ai = pakai = piḷavu, piriviṉai.
	paku + am = pakkam - pakkal. pakkam = pakuti, titi.
	paku + ti = pakuti - pāti. paku + pu = pakuppu.
	pakku - paṅku.
	pāku - pākam. pākkam = pakkam, ūrppakuti.
	pāku - pāṅku - pāṅkar. pāṅku = pakkam, taṉmai.
	pāṅku + aṉ = pāṅkaṉ.
(b)	paku - pā. pātti = pakukkappaṭṭa ceyp pākam. pātīṭu = pakuttal.
(c)	paku - vaku. vaku + ai = vakai. vaku + pu = vakuppu.
(d)	pakir - vakir.
67. peḷ
	peḷ = virumpu, kātali.
	peṭpu = viruppam.
	peḷ - peṇ = virumpappaṭum pāl.
	peḷ + tai = peṭṭai - peṭai - pēṭai - pēṭu
	pēṭu + aṉ = pēṭaṉ = āṇṭaṉmaiyuḷḷa peṇ.
	pēṭu + i = pēṭi = peṇṭaṉmaiyuḷḷa āṇ.
	peṇ - piṇā - piṇavu - piṇaval. piṇā - piṇai.
	peṇ - pēṇ = virumpu, virumpip pātukā.
68. poḷ
	poḷ = tuḷaiyiṭu.
	poṇṭāṉ = eli pottuk kiḷampum vaḷai.
(a)	poḷi = veṭṭu, varappu.
	poḷ - pol.
	pol + am = pollam = oṭṭai.
	poḷḷāp piḷḷaiyār = pollāp piḷḷaiyār.
	pokku = tuḷaiyuḷḷatu, uḷḷīṭaṟṟa tāṉiyam, poy
		pokkuvāy = pallaṟṟa vāy
	pokku + aṇam = pokkaṇam = pai.
	poy = uḷḷīṭaṟṟatu, meyyallātatu.
	po = tuḷaiyiṭu. pottal = tuḷai.
	poḷ - pōḻ = piḷa, veṭṭu.
	poḻil = veṭṭappaṭuvatu, cōlai.
(b)	pōḻ = veṭṭu, tuṇṭu. pōḻtu = iruḷaip piḷakkum cūriyaṉ.
	pōḻtu - poḻutu - pōtu = vēḷai.
69. pō
(a)	pō = cel. pōtu = pō. pōku = pō.
	pōkku = cellal, ātaravu.
	pōkku - pōṅku = pōkum muṟai, mātiri.
(b)	pōtum = cellum, vēṇṭiya aḷavākum.
	pōtiya, pōnta = aḷavāṉa, pōtumāṉa.
	pōku = nīḷ.
	pōtu = virinta arumpu.
	pōntai = virinta paṉai ōlai.
	pōntai - pottakam - puttakam.
70. vaḷ
	vaḷ = vaḷai.
	vaḷḷam = vaṭṭak kalam.
	vaḷākam = cūḻnta iṭam, vaḷār = vaḷainta pirampu.
	vaḷai = vaḷaiyal, vaḷainta caṅku, vaḷainta uttaram, 				vaṭṭat tuvāram.
	vaḷai + al = vaḷaiyal, vaḷai + vi = vaḷaivi.
	vaḷai + am = vaḷaiyam.
	vaṭṭam = vaḷaiyam, vaṭṭakkācu, vaṭṭi, pakuti.
	vaṭṭi = vaṭṭakkācu, kaṭaṉukkuc celuttum kācu, vaḷainta peṭṭi.
	vaṭṭil = vaṭṭak kalam.
	vaṭṭu = vaṭṭamāṉa cil.
	vaṭṭakai, vaṭṭāram = iṭappakuti.
	vaṇaṅku = uṭampu vaḷai.
	vaḻaṅku = vaḷaintu koṭu
	vaṇar = vaḷainta yāḻuṟuppu.
	vaṇaṅku - vāṅku - vaṅku. vaṅki = vaḷaintatu.
	vaṇṭi = vaṭṭac cakkaram, cakkarattaiyuṭaiya cakaṭam.
	vaṇṭu = vaḷaiyal, vaṭṭamāṉa vaṇṭu.
	vaṇṭi - paṇṭi - pāṇṭi - pāṇṭil.
	pāṇṭi = vaṭṭāṭal. pāṇṭil = vaṭṭak kiṇṇam, 				uruṭciyāṉa erutu.
	pāṇṭiyaṉ = vīraṉ.
	vaḷi = vaḷaintu vīcum kāṟṟu.
	vāḷi = vaḷaiyam, vaḷainta piṭi, vaḷaintuviḻum ampu
	vāṇam = vaḷaiyum veṭivakai. vāṇam - pāṇam = ampu.
71. veḷ
	veḷ = veḷḷaiyāku.
	veḷi = veḷḷaiyāṉa iṭam. veṭṭa = veḷḷaiyāṉa.
	veḷḷāḷaṉ = veṇkaḷamaṉ.
	veḷḷāṭṭi = veḷḷāḷappeṇ, vēlaikkāri.
	veḷḷari = veṇkōṭuḷḷa kāy.
	veḷḷai = veḷḷait tuṇi, cuṇṇāmpu, kaḷḷamaṟṟavaṉ.
	veḷḷanti = kaḷḷamiṉmai.
	veḷḷam = veḷḷaiyāṉa putu nīr.
	veḷḷi = veḷḷaiyāṉa ulōkam, naṭcattiram.
	veḷḷilai - veṟṟilai.
	veḷḷil - viḷa - viḷā - viḷavu = veḷḷōṭuḷḷa paḻamaram.
	veḷil - veḷiṟu = veḷḷaimaram.
	veḷu = veḷḷaiyākku, tuvai, aṭi.
	veḷku - veṭku = nāṇattāl mukam veḷu.
	viḷaṅku = oḷiviṭu, pulaṉāku.
	viḷakku = viḷaṅkac ceyvatu.
72. vēku.
	vē = vēku, eri.
	vēkam = viraivu, kaṭumai.
	ventai = venta kīrai.
	vēmpu = cūṭāṉa paḻam paḻuppatu. allatu vēṉilil taḻaippatu.
	vēṉal = veppam, vēṉil = kōṭai.
	vekkai = vēṉal, veppu = cūṭu, veppu - veppam.
	vempu = veyiliṟ kāy. vempal = kāyntapaḻam.
	vētu = cūṭu, ottaṭam, vetuvetuppu = cūṭu.
	vetumpu = cuṭu, vētaṉai = nōvu.
	vemmai = cūṭu, kaṭumai, viruppam.
	veyyil - veyil = veppamāṉa au
73. vēḷ
	vēḷ = virumpu.
	vēḷ - vēṇṭu = virumpu, keñcik kēḷ.
	vēḷ - vēṇ + avā = vēṇavā.
	vēṭkai = viruppam, tākam, viṭāy = viruppam, tākam.
	vēḷvi = oṉṟai virumpic ceyyum yākam.
	vēḷāṇmai = virumpic ceyyum upacāram.
	vēḷāḷaṉ = uḻavaṉ, vēḷir = oru kulat talaivaṉ, 				kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ.
	vēḷāṇ = uḻavar kuṭi. vēḷāṉ = oru paṭṭam
	vēḷāṭṭi = vēḷāḷap peṇ. vēḷam = vēḷāḷap peṇṭir 			ciṟaikkaḷam.
	vēṇmakaṉ - vēṇmāṉ = kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ.
vēṭṭam, vēṭṭai = virumpi vilaṅkaip piṭittal. 
vēṭṭuvaṉ, vēṭuvaṉ, vēṭaṉ = vēṭṭaiyāṭupavaṉ.
2
pōlikai yuruppaṭikaḷ
1. a (eḻuttum collum colluṟuppum kuṟiyum)
1. eḻuttu
a1	a. pe. n. tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ mutaleḻuttu; first letter of the Tamil alphabet.
vakai. uyirkkuṟil : short vowel.
colvakai : aṅkāttalāl oru māttirai yaḷavolikkum aḵṟiṇaic cevippulap poruḷākiya taṟcuṭṭolip peyar.
vēṟṟumaippāṭu. 2ām vakai, 3ām pirivu. e-ṭu : avvai, avvāl, avviṟku, avviṉ, avvatu, avvil.
2. col
a2   a. pe; n. 1 aḻaku; beauty. "avvāy vaḷarpiṟai cūṭi" (perum pāṇ. 412), 2. civaṉ, Siva. "āru maṟiyā rakāra mavaṉeṉṟu" (tiruman. 1751). 3. tirumāl Vishnu "av veṉ coṟporuḷāvāṉ" (pākava. cicupā. 20). 4. nāṉ mukaṉ, Brahma. (takka yākap. 65, urai), 5. cukku; dried ginger (pari. aka.) 6. tippili; long pepper. (pari. aka.)
a3   a. pe. e; adj. puṟaccuṭṭu: demon. prefix: a = anta. 1. cēymaic cuṭṭu; pref. to nouns, expressing remoteness. e-ṭu: ap paiyaṉ. 2. muṟpeyarc cuṭṭu; pref. to nouns, referring to their antecedents. e-ṭu : paṇṭait tamiḻa kattai āṇṭavar cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar. am mūvēn tarum iṉṟillai. 3. ulakaṟi cuṭṭu; pref. expressing world-wide eminence. 'attam perumāṉ' (cīvaka. 221)
3. colluṟuppu (i; ind)
a4   a, akaccuṭṭu: demon. base : mutaṉilai - 1. cēymaic cuṭṭu; base of the dem. pron. expressing the remote person or thing
	   	e-ṭu : avaṉ, aṅku, aṉṟu. 2. muṟpeyarc cuṭṭu; 		base of the dem. pron. referring to its antecedent. 		e-ṭu : ōrūril ōraracaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉukku iru 		makkaḷ. 3. ulakaṟi cuṭṭu: base of the dem. pron. 		expressing world-wide eminence. e-ṭu: "avaṉaṉṟi 		ōr aṇuvum acaiyātu" (paḻamoḻi). 4. potu 			nilaiccuṭṭu: base of the dem. pron. expressing 			generality or indefiniteness.  e-ṭu "avaravar 			akkaṟaikku avaravar pāṭupaṭuvār" (paḻamoḻi).
a5		a. iṉmai yaṉmai maṟutalaip poruḷil varum muṉ ṉoṭṭu pref. implying negation, privation or contrariety.
	e-ṭu : iṉmai - avalam = valam illātatu 			(tuṉpam), tuyaram.
		aṉmai - akālam = kālam allātatu.
		maṟutalai - acuraṉ = curaṉukku māṟāṉavaṉ.
a6   	a. oru coṟcāriyai; a euphonic augment.
	   	e-ṭu : vaṇṇārappēṭṭai.
a7   	a. meyyeḻuttuc cāriyai; an enunciative augment.
	e-ṭu:
	"meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum"	(tol. 46)
	"valleḻut teṉpa kacaṭa tapaṟa"	(tol. 19)
a8   	a. ōr acaiccol; an expletive generally in poetry.
	e-ṭu : "taṉvaḻiya kāḷai" (cīvaka.494).
a9   	a rakarattil toṭaṅkum cila camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷiṉ muṉṉoṭṭu; euphonic prothesis of some sanskrit words beginning with ra. e-ṭu: ratna - arataṉam.
a10  	irakkam, viyappu, tuyaram mutaliyaṉa uṇarttum oru kuṟippuc colluṟuppu: a part of an interjection expressing pity, wonder, grief etc.
"uṇṇā ṇoḷiniṟā ṉōṅku pukaḻceyyāṉ
tuṉṉaruṅ kēḷir tuyarkaḷaiyāṉ - koṉṉē
vaḻaṅkāṉ poruḷkāt tiruppāṉēl a ā
iḻantāṉeṉ ṟeṇṇap paṭum"	(nālaṭi. 9)
eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷil 'a ā' irakkap poruḷil vantatu.
a11		a. oru palaviṉpāṟ peyarīṟu; a neut. pl. noun suf.
		e-ṭu: cila pala.
a12	a. āṟām vēṟṟumai aḵṟiṇaip paṉmai yurupu genitive neut. pl. ending e-ṭu: eṉa kaikaḷ.
a13	a. oru palaviṉpāl viṉaimuṟṟīṟu; a neut. pl. verbal ending. e-ṭu: vantaṉa. vanta. periyaṉa. periya.
a14	a. oru viyaṅkōḷ viṉai yīṟu; an opt. verbal ending. e-ṭu: varappuyara. viyaṅkōḷ viṉaiyellām muṟṟē.
a15	a. iṟantakāla nikaḻkālat terinilaip peyarecca vīṟu a rel. participial ending. e-ṭu: vanta, varukiṉṟa.
a16	a. oru (kuṟippup) peyarecca vīṟu; an adj. ending. e-ṭu, nalla, ciṟiya.
a17	a. oru nikaḻkāla (etirkālap poruṭṭu) viṉaiyecca vīṟu; an infinitive suf. e-ṭu: uṇṇa.
a18	a. oru collākka īṟu; formative particle.
e-ṭu: kul-kal-kala, piḷ-piḷa.
a19	a. cila peyarkaḷiṉ ākāra vīṟṟuk kuṟukkam; shortening of 'an' ending certain nouns e-ṭu: irā-ira, kaḷā-kaḷa, nilā-nila, puṟā-puṟa.
a20	a. kuṟilil iṟum vaṇṇappāṭṭuc cīrkaḷiṉ allatu acai kaḷiṉ vaṇṇak kuḻippu vāypāṭṭīṟu; ending of rhythmatic patterns of metrical feet and syllebles ending in a short vowel. e-ṭu: taṉa, taṉaṉa, tāṉa, tatta, tanta, tayya, taṉatta, taṉanta.
a21	a. neṭuṅkaṇakkup peyaruṟuppu: part of a name of the alphabet. 'aā' = neṭuṅkaṇakku. e-ṭu: uṉakku 'aā' teriyumā? itai 'a'ṉa 'ā'vaṉṉā eṉṟatu paṇṭai vaḻakku.
a22	a. ukaramutal tiripu collākkattil ukaramutal akara mutalākat tirivatuṇṭu. e-ṭu: ukai (celuttu) - akai, kuṭṭai - kaṭṭai, kuṭumpu - kaṭumpu, kuṭai(tal) - kaṭai(tal), curi (vaḷaiyal) - cari, tuḷir - taḷir, tuṇai- taṉai (ettuṇai-ettaṉai, varuntuṇaiyum - varuntaṉaiyum), nurai (veṇmai) - narai, puri - pari(vaḷai), puram (uyarnta iṭam allatu kaṭṭiṭam, e-ṭu: paraveḷi, kōpuram) - param - varam -varaṉ (mēlāṉa vīṭṭunilam), muṭaṅku - maṭaṅku, muṭi - maṭi (iṟa) muyaṅku - mayaṅku.
it tiripiṉāl, cila coṟkaḷiṉ mūlattaiyum cila coṟkaḷaip puṉaiyum vakaiyaiyum aṟiyalām.
4. kuṟi
a23	a.1. eṭṭu eṉṉum eṇṇiṉ kuṟiyāka 'a' cuḻiyiṉṟi eḻutappaṭuvatu; symbol of the number eight, written without the loop.
	2. 	aimpuḷḷuḷ (pañcapaṭciyuḷ) oṉṟāy vallūṟṟaik kuṟikkum eḻuttu; letter representing the vulture one of the five astrologically important birds.
a24	a. moḻi mutal eḻuttukaḷuḷ oṉṟu. e-ṭu: avaṉ
varalāṟu
1. eḻuttu :	ā-a. akaram ākārattiṉ kuṟukkam. uyi rolikaḷellām perumpālum iyaṟkaiyāka neṭilākavē tōṉṟip piṉpu kuṟukiṉa. itaṉ viḷakkattai neṭuṅ kaṇakku eṉṉum urup paṭiyiṟ kāṇka.
2. col :	am = aḻaku. am-a2.
a2 = eḻuttukaṭku mutalākiya akaram pōl ulakaṅkaṭku mutalākiya iṟaivaṉ.
"akara mutala veḻuttellā māti
ðèõù¢ ºîø¢«ø »ô°."	(kuṟaḷ. 1)
iṟaivaṉaik kuṟiñci nilattuc cēyōṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṉar civaṉ eṉṟum mullai nilattu māyōṉ vaḻipāṭṭiṉar tirumāl eṉṟum, kuṟittaṉar. ki.mu. 1500-ām āṇṭupōl nāvalan tēyattiṟkuṭ pukunta ciṟu teyva vēḷvimata āriyar, civaneṟiyum tirumāl neṟiyum ākiya iru tūya tamiḻ mataṅkaḷaiyum āriyappaṭuttaṟku muttirumēṉi (tirimūrtti)k koḷkaiyaip pukutti, nāṉmukaṉ (piramaṉ) eṉṉum paṭaipput teyvattaip putuvatākap paṭaittu, at teyvattaiyum akarattāṟ kuṟittaṉar.
cukku, tippili eṉṉum maruttuvac carakkukaḷ, ētēṉumoru karaṇiyam paṟṟi akaram eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruntirukkalām, ataip piṟkālattār 'a' (a2) eṉṟu kuṟittirukkalām.
(puṟaccuṭṭu ā = anta. "āyīriyala" eṉṟu (tol. 17) ceyyuḷ vaḻakkilum, āyāḷ (malaiyāḷam), āvūru (teluṅku) eṉṟu ulaka vaḻakkilum, ākāram collākavē vaḻaṅkivarutal kāṇka.
ā - a3.
3. colluṟuppu
ā(akaccuṭṭu)-a4 . ā-āku-āṅku-aṅku, ātu-atu.
al - a5. ō.nā. nal - na.
a6	(coṟcāriyai) peyarecca vīṟā yirukkalām.
a7	(eḻuttuc cāriyai) neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ mutaṉmaiyum paluk keḷimaiyum paṟṟiyatu.
a8	(acaiccol) kuṟippuc peyarecca vīṟāyirukkalām. acaic coṟkaḷellām poruḷ kuṉṟiya allatu iḻanta coṟkaḷē. 
a9 (ayaṟcol muṉṉoṭṭu) palukkeḷimaiyum āṭciyum paṟṟiyatu. palukku=uccarippu
a10	(irakkak kuṟippu) iyaṟkai paṟṟiyatu. ā - a. o.nā : E. ha. hah.
a11	(palaviṉpāṟ peyarīṟu) cuṭṭāṭci paṟṟiyatu.
a12	(āṟām vēṟṟumai aḵṟiṇaip paṉmaiyurupu) palaviṉ pāṟ kuṟippu viṉaimuṟṟīṟu. kaikaḷ eṉa- eṉa kaikaḷ (coṉ muṟai māṟṟu).
a13	(palaviṉpāl viṉaimuṟṟīṟu) cuṭṭāṭci paṟṟiyatu.
a14	(viyaṅkōḷ viṉaimuṟṟīṟu) allīṟṟut toḻiṟpeyariṉ īṟukēṭu. ceyyal - ceyya ceyal - ceya.

a15 (iṟantakāla nikaḻkālat terinilaip peyareccavīṟu) cuṭṭāṭci paṟṟiyatu.
cey = ceykai. cey + atu = ceyyatu - ceytu = ceykaiyu ṭaiya - vaṉ - vaḷ - var - tu - vai. ceykaiyuṭaiyavaṉ - ceytavaṉ = ceytāṉ. viṉaiyuṭaimai viṉai muṭintamaiyaiyuṇarttum.
uṇṭu eṉṉum oṉṟaṉpāṟ kuṟippu viṉaimuṟṟu, iṉṟu irutiṇai īreṇ mūviṭa aimpāṟ potuvāy vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟē, atu eṉṉum cuṭṭuc collai īṟākak koṇṭa ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu iṟantakāla viṉaic collum, mutaṟkālattil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
uḷ + atu = uḷḷatu. uḷ + tu (atu) = uṇṭu.
ceytu + a = ceyta = ceyta - vaṉ - vaḷ - var - atu - vai āṉa anta.
ceytu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉaiccol mutaṟkālattil muṟṟu viṉaiyākavum, piṉṉar muṟṟu viṉaiyum eccamumākavum vaḻaṅki, ataṉpiṉ aimpālīṟu peṟṟa kālan toṭaṅki eccamākavē vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. ceytu eṉṉum iṟantakāla viṉaiccol pōṉṟē, ceykiṉṟu eṉṉum nikaḻkāla viṉaiccollum akaravīṟu peṟṟup peyareccamākum.
ceykiṉṟu + a = ceykiṉṟa - ceykiṟa.
ceykiṉṟu eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉaic col eccap poruḷil vaḻakkaṟṟatu.
viṉaiccol vakaikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ iṭainilaikaḷum, īṟukaḷum, avaṟṟiṟkuriya iṭaṅkaḷil virivāka viḷakkappeṟum.
a16   (kuṟippup peyareccavīṟu) cuṭṭāṭci paṟṟiyatu.
a17   (nikaḻkāla viṉaiyeccavīṟu) allīṟṟut toḻiṟ peya riṉ īṟukēṭṭāl nērntatu.
ceyyal (vēṇṭum) - ceyya (vēṇṭum).

a18	poruḷ vēṟupāṭu paṟṟiyatu.
a19	palukkeḷimai nōkkiyatu.
a20	akarattiṉ mutaṉmaiyum māttiraiyum paṟṟiyatu.

4. kuṟi
a23	(eṭṭeṉṉum eṇkuṟiyum vallūṟṟiṉ kuṟiyum)
eṇvakaippaṭṭa poruḷiṉ allatu poruṭṭokutiyiṉ peyar mutaleḻuttākavum, vallūṟṟiṉ peyar oṉṟaṉ mutaleḻut tākavum irukkalām. irupeyarum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa pōlum.
ciṟappuk kuṟippu
eḻuttukkaḷai yellām uyirum meyyum eṉak kūṟupaṭutti yum, uyireḻuttukkaḷaiyum meyyeḻuttukkaḷaiyum piṟappum oliyum paṟṟi muṟaippaṭuttiyum, uyirukkum meykkum pōṉṟē uyirmey kaṭkum vēṟu varivaṭivamaittum, vaṇṇamālai (alphabet) mutaṉ mutalāka amaikkap peṟṟatu tamiḻilēyē. uyirum meyyum maṭṭuṅ koṇṭatu kuṟuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, avaṟṟoṭu uyirmeyyuṅ koṇ ṭatu neṭuṅkaṇakku eṉṟum, peyarpeṟum. iruvakaik kaṇakkilum akaramē tamiḻiṉ mutaleḻuttām.
vēta āriyar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukunta piṉṉum, nīṇṭakālam eḻuttiṉṟit tam mutaṉūlākiya vētattai vāymoḻiyākavē vaḻaṅki vantataṉāl, aḵtu 'eḻutāk kiḷavi' eṉappaṭṭatu. tamiḻarōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉpē, mutaliṟ kiranta veḻuttaiyum piṉpu tēvanākari yaiyum 'avar' amaittuk koṇṭaṉar. iṟṟait tamiḻeḻuttiṟkum acōkaṉ kalveṭṭup pirāmi yeḻuttiṟkum yātoru toṭarpu millai. itaṉ viḷakkattai 'neṭuṅkaṇakku' eṉṉum uruppaṭiyiṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
ki.mu. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarāṉa tiruvaḷḷuvar 'akaramutala tamiḻ eḻuttellām' eṉṉātu "akara mutala eḻuttellām" eṉṟu potup paṭak kūṟi yiruppatāl, teṉṉāṭṭut tamiḻeḻuttōṭu ataṉ vaḻip paṭṭa kiranta veḻuttaiyum, vaṭanāṭṭup pirāmi yeḻuttaiyum avar aṟintiruttal vēṇṭum.
'kuvā kuvā'veṉṟu aḻuvatākac collappaṭum kuḻavi  vāyiṉ mutaloliyai 'aā, aā' eṉṟu aḻum oliyākavum koḷḷalā meṉiṉum, atu muḻaittal moḻiyaic (inarticulate speech) cērnta voliyākalāṉ iḻaittal moḻiyaic (articulate speech) cērnta aā voliyākātu. ata ṉālēyē akara mutala moḻiyellām eṉṉātu "akara mutala eḻuttellām" eṉak kūṟiṉār tiruvaḷḷuvar. piṉīciyam, epirēyam, arapi, kirēkkam, ilattīṉam, āṅkilam mutaliya mēlai moḻikaḷiṉ kuṟuṅkaṇakkilum akaramē mutaleḻuttām. āṅkila akaram  tamiḻakarattai yottu nērākavum olikkum; atai yovvātu kōṇaiyākavum olikkum. ak kōṇaiyoli, vaṭārk kāṭṭu āmpūr makkaḷ kāy eṉṉum colliluḷḷa ākārattaiyo lippatu pōṉṟatu. avvoli centamiḻiṟ koḷḷappaṭātu. eḻuttiṟ kuriya nāṉilaikaḷuḷ iṟutinilai yaṭainta ellā moḻiyeḻuttuk kaḷum, akarattaiyē mutalāka vuṭaiyaṉa. 
ceṉṉaip pa. ka. ka. ta. akaramutalic cīrtiruttam
ce.pa.ka.ka.ta. akaramutali,
(1)	vaṭacol muṉvait teḻutappaṭum akara muṉṉoṭṭiṟku raṅkam - araṅkam eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyum.
(2) 	iṉmai aṉmai maṟutalaip poruḷil varum akarattai 'na' eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi avviyayattiṉ tiripeṉṟu kūṟi, ataṟku muṟaiyē arūpam, appirāmaṇaṉ, atarmam eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyum uḷḷatu.
araṅkam eṉṉum col aṟukkappaṭṭatu eṉṉum vērp poruḷai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu āṟṟiṭaik kuṟaiyaik kuṟik kum tūya teṉcol eṉṟum, srīraṅkam eṉa vaḻaṅkum tirunakariṉ paḻampeyar tiru varaṅkam eṉṉum teṉcol vaṭivē yeṉṟum, araṅkam eṉṉum teṉ collē vaṭamoḻiyil raṅka eṉat tirinta teṉṟum, araṅkam eṉṉum uruppaṭiyil veḷḷiṭai malaiyeṉa viḷakkappeṟum.
al eṉṉum teṉcollē, aṉ eṉattirintu vaṭamoḻiyil 'na' eṉa ilakkaṇap pōliyāka māṟiyuḷḷatai, al eṉṉum urup paṭiyiṟ kaṇṭu teḷika.
iṉi, akarac cāriyaikku eṭuttukkāṭṭiya 'tamiḻap piḷḷai' eṉṉum kūṭṭuc collaiyum tamiḻ + piḷḷai eṉap pakukkātu tamiḻaṉ + piḷḷai 
yeṉap pakuttu, nilaimoḻiyīṟu keṭṭu varumoḻi mutal valimikka puṇarcciyākak koḷvatē takkatām eṉavum aṟika. 
ittakaiya maṟuppellām, iṉivarum uruppaṭikaḷil tiruttam eṉṉum talaippiṉ kīḻēyē kuṟikkappaṭum.
meyyeḻuttuc cāriyai. oru collākka īṟu, cilapeyarkaḷiṉ ākāra vīṟṟuk kuṟukkam, vaṇṇak kuḻippu vāypāṭṭīṟu, neṭuṅ kaṇakkup peyaruṟuppu eṉṉum aimporuḷum ceṉṉai yakara mutaliyiṟ kuṟikkap peṟavillai.
2. avarai
avarai avarai.
vaḻaṅkum iṭam - tamiḻakam.
col vakai : aḵṟiṇai ōraṟivuyirp poruṭpeyar.
iyal viḷakkam - perumpāṉmai paccai niṟamum ciṟupāṉmai veṇṇiṟamum cenniṟamuḷḷatum, perumpālum taṭṭai vaṭivāṉatum, iru viralam (inches) mutal aṟuviralam varai nīṇṭu pala vakaippaṭṭi ruppatum, vīṭṭup puṟaṅkaḷil viḷaivikkappaṭuvatum, naḷi (kārttikai) cilai (mārkaḻi) cuṟava (tai) mātaṅkaḷil ūr koṭiyākavum (creeper) ciṟappāka ivar koṭiyākavum (climber) paṭarkoṭiyiṟ kāyppatum kaṟi vakaiyākac camaikkap paṭuvatum uṭal nalattiṟkēṟṟatum, cuvaiyuḷ ḷatumāṉa kāy vakai a kind of bean including many varieties.
maṟupeyarkaḷum vaḻaṅkum iṭamum:

poṅkutiri tōṭattōr puṇcurattōr - taṅkaḷukkum

itaṉ poruḷ - vitai mutirāta veḷḷaiyavaraip piñcu, irā vuṇaviṟkum maruntuṇpavarkkum ūtai (vātam) mutaliya munnāṭik kuṟṟam, puṇ, kāyccal viḻikkuḷ mutirnta kōḻaip pillam ākiya nōyuṭaiyārkkum nallatām.
vēṟṟumaippāṭu. mutalvakai. e-ṭu : avaraiyai, avaraiyāl, avaraikku, avaraiyiṉ, avaraiyatu, avaraiyil.

collamaivu
'am' vēr allatu aṭi mutaṉilai. 'aval', amal eṉpataṉ tiripāki mēṉ mutaṉilaiyāka niṟkum toḻilākupeyar. 'ai' paṇpi yīṟu. lakaram rakaramāṉatu pōlit tiripu.
col varalāṟu :
ammutal = amuṅkutal, amukkutal.
am - ammi = amukki yaraikkuṅ kal.
am - (amu) - (amuku) - amuṅku - amukku - amukkam
amukku - amukkal - amukkalāṉ = koppuḷaṅkaḷai amuṅkac ceyyum taḻai.
am - amiḻ - āḻ - aḻuntu - aḻuttu - aḻuttam.
amukkuvataṉālum aḻuttuvataṉālum ōriṭam paḷḷamākum; oru poruḷ taṭṭaiyākum.
am - amal - aval = 1. paḷḷam. "Üõô¤ö¤ò¤Âñ¢ ñ¤¬ê «òø¤Âñ¢." (puṟam. 102 : 3). 2. viḷainilam. 3. kuḷam. 4. taṭṭaiyāka iṭitta nellarici allatu kamparici. paḷḷak karuttiṉiṉṟu kuḷḷak karuttun tōṉṟum.
o.nā : paḷ - paḷḷam. paḷ - paḷḷai = 1. kuḷḷam. 2. paḷḷaiyāṭu. paḷḷaiyaṉ = kuṟukit taṭittavaṉ. paḷḷaiyāṭu = kuḷḷamāṉa āṭu. kuḷḷak karuttiṉiṉṟu cappaik karuttut tōṉṟum.
ñ - õ., pōli o.nā : cemmai - cevvai.
cuma - cumal - cuval = cumakkun tōṭpaṭṭai.
aval - (avalai) - avarai = cappaiyāṉa allatu taṭṭai yāṉa kāyvakai.
o.nā : ayil - ayilai - ayirai.
la - ra, pōli.
avarai vakaikaḷiṟ cila uruṇṭu tiraṇṭiruppiṉum, perum pāṉmai nōkki avai vilakkākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. mēlum, piñcu nilaiyilēyē camaikkumāṟu maruttuva nūlkaḷ kūṟuvatāl, ellā vakaikaḷum piñcu nilaiyil cappaiyākavē yiruttalai nōkkuka.
ovvoru poruḷum ataṉ ciṟappiyalpu paṟṟiyē peyar peṟuvatu marapu. vāḻai, kattari, muruṅkai, veṇṭai, pūcaṇi, curai, pīrkku, puṭalai, pākal mutaliya piṟa kāyvakaikaḷ ellāvaṟṟōṭum oppunōkki, avarai yoṉṟē taṭṭaiyā yiruttalaik kaṇṭaṟika.
paḷḷam eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ mēlmaṭṭattiṉ tāḻvu; kuṭṭai eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ uyarattiṉ tāḻvu; cappai eṉpatu oṉṟaṉ tiṇ ṇattiṉ allatu puṭaippiṉ 
tāḻvu. im muk karuttum ōriṉappaṭṭaṉa. āḻamillāta kiṇṇam taṭṭam eṉap peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
poruḷvakai :
(1)	āṭṭuk kompavarai = āṭṭuk kompupōl vaṭi vuḷḷa avarai; kind of bean that resembles goat's horn in shape
(2)	ārāl mīṉavarai = ārāl mīṉ pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷa avarai; kind of bean that resembles sand - eel in shape.
(3)	āṉaik kātavarai = āṉaik kātu pōl vaṭivuḷḷa avarai; kind of bean that resembles elephant's ear in shape.
(4)	kaṇuvavarai = koṭiyiṉ kaṇukkaḷiṟ kāykkum avarai; kind of bean that brings forth fruits at joints also.
(5)	koḻuppavarai = kuṭṭaiyākak koḻutta cataiyuḷḷa avarai; kind of bean that is short and fleshy.
(6)	kōḻiyavarai (kōḻikkā lavarai) = kōḻi viral pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷa avarai; canavalia gladiata
(7)	civappavarai - cevvavarai = cenniṟa avarai; a red variety of bean, lablab cultratus.
(8)	ciṟṟavarai - maṇiyavarai = a small variety of bean.
(9)	tīvāntara vavarai = kīḻait tīviṉiṉṟu vanta avarai; a bean from the eastern islands (w).
(10)	nakaravarai - dolichos rugosus (W)
(11)	pālavarai - veḷḷavarai; a white variety of bean,
"avaraik koḻuṅkoṭi viḷarkkāy
«è£ì¢ðî ñ£è."		(puṟam. 120: 10-11)
(12)	pēravarai - a large variety of bean.
(13)	muṟukkavarai = tirukal muṟukalākavuḷḷa avarai; psopha carpus tetragonolobus.
iṉapporuḷkaḷ
(1)	kappal avarai = french beans
(2)	kāṭṭavarai = koḻuppavarai pōṉṟatāyuḷḷa moccai (cēlam vaḻakku); wild bean, lablab vulgaris.
vīṭṭup puṟattil viḷaivikkappaṭum avaraiyai nōkki moccai kāṭ ṭavarai eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl, avaraikku vīṭṭavarai eṉa ōr aṭaimoḻip peyar peṟappaṭum.
vīṭṭavaraikkum kāṭṭavaraikkum vēṟupāṭu. 
	vīṭṭavarai	kāṭṭavarai
(1)	vīṭṭup puṟattil	kāṭṭup puṟattil
	viḷaikkappaṭuvatu	viḷaikkappaṭuvatu.
(2)	koṭiyākap paṭarvatu	kuttuc ceṭiyāka vaḷarvatu.
(3)	iḷaṅkāy kaṟiyākac	muṟṟiya kāy avittuc 
	camaikkappaṭuvatu	tiṉṉappaṭuvatu.
(4)	tōl melliyatu	tōl valliyatu.
(3) ayalnāṭṭu (cīmai) avarai = marutaṅkāy pōṉṟa vaṭivuḷḷatum nīṇṭatumāṉa veḷināṭṭuk kāyvakai; a kind of foreign vegetable. ayal nāṭṭai allatu mēl nāṭṭaip potu makkaḷ 'cīmai' eṉṉum vaṭacollāṟ kuṟippar. iṅku vaṭacol eṉṟatu vaṭanāṭṭuc collai.
(4) cīṉiyavarai - kottavarai - kuttavarai = kottuk kottākak kāykkum kāyvakai; a species of pulse whose fruits are in bunches.
(5) cuṭalaiyavarai = cuṭukāṭṭup pakkattil iyaṟkaiyāka viḷaivatum, cīṉiyavarai pōṉṟatum, uṇṇap paṭātatumāṉa kāy vakai; a species of inedible bean growing wildly near burial or burning grounds. itu pēyavarai yeṉavum paṭum.
(6) pāṭavarai - vāḷavarai - tampaṭṭavarai - cāṭṭavarai = vāḷpōl nīṇṭu paṭṭaiyāka virukkum kāyvakai; sword-bean, canavalia ensiformis.

avaraik kāy eṉpatu malaiyāḷattil avarakka eṉat tirintuḷḷatu. amarakka eṉpatu avarakka eṉpataṉ tirivu.
tuḷuc coṟkaḷuḷ rakaramuḷḷavai oruvakaiyaiyum ṭakaramuḷ ḷavai maṟṟoru vakaiyaiyum kuṟippaṉavākap parō emaṉō tiraviṭa akaramutaliyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tuḷu akaramutaliyil aṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟikkappaṭavillai.
iṇaimoḻi:
avarai tuvarai.
ciṟappuk kuṟippu
"avarai koyyuna rāra māntum"	(puṟam. 215: 5).
"ciṟukoṭik koḷḷē poṟikiḷa ravaraiyoṭu"	(puṟam. 335:5)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaṭikaḷ, paṇṭaik kālattil avaraiyum kāṭṭil, moccaipōla viḷaikkappaṭṭa teṉṟō, moccaiyum avarai yeṉap paṭṭateṉṟō, karuta iṭantarum.
koṭṭaiyavarai yeṉṟu patārtta kuṇa cintāmaṇi kuṟitti ruppatu. oru taṉi vakaiyākat teriyavillai.
cīrtiruttam:
ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka tamiḻ akaramutali, kōḻiyavaraiyai vāḷavarai (sword-bean) yeṉṟu kuṟittuḷḷatu.
ārāl mīṉavarai, āṉaikkātavarai, koḻuppavarai, cuṭalai avarai mutaliya peyarkaḷ atiṟ kuṟikkappeṟa villai.
mēl nāṭṭut tamiḻaṟiñar tam aṟiyāmaiyāl avaraikkāy eṉpataṉ tiripāṉa avarakka, amarakka eṉṉum coṟkaḷaiyum avaraiyaik kuṟikkum taṉic coṟkaḷākak kuṟittuḷḷaṉar.
3. iñci
col : iñci
vaḻakkiṭam :	iñci1 - ilakkiyam
	iñci2 - tamiḻakam
colvakai : peyarccol
iñci1 = kaṭṭiṭa vaṭivāṉa iṭappeyar
iñci2 = aḵṟiṇai ōraṟivuyirp poruṭpeyar.
vēṟṟumaippāṭṭu vakai.
mutalvakai (cāriyai yillatu), e-ṭu: iñciyai, iñciyāl, iñcikku, iñciyiṉ, iñciyatu, iñciyil.
iyal viḷakkam
iñci1 - cempurukkic cāntāka vārttu iṟukak kaṭṭiya tiṇ ṇiya kōṭṭai matil vakai.
iñci2 - pittattaip pōkkuvatum, maruntukaḷilum kaṟivakai kaḷilum perumpālum kūṭṭuc carakkākac cērkkappaṭuvatum, kārppuc cuvaiyuḷḷatumāṉa, kiḻaṅkuḷḷa pūṇṭuvakai.
poruḷum āṭci mēṟkōḷum
iñci1, pe. tiṇṇiya kōṭṭai matil vakai
"koṭuṅka ṇiñci"	(patiṟṟup. 16:1).
"uyarvakalan tiṇmai yarumaiyin nāṉkiṉ
amaivaraṇ eṉṟuraikku nūl"	(743)
eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷiṟ kuṟittuḷḷa tiṇmai eṉṉum vakaippaṭi. "cempupuṉain tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum puricai" yē (puṟam. 201) iñci eṉṉum matil vakaiyākum.
iñci2, pe. 1. iñcip pūṇṭu; ginger-plant. "mañcaḷum iñciyum mayaṅkaril valayattu" (cilap 10:74). 2. iñcik kiḻaṅku; ginger-root.
āṭci mēṟkōḷ :
iñci1 - "cempiṭṭuc ceyta viñcit tirunakarc celvan tēṟi" (kamparā. yutta. 160) 
ce.co.pi. akaramutaliyil iṉṉum eṇ mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭap peṟum virivañci avai iṅku viṭappaṭṭuḷa.
iñci vakai:
iñci māṅkāy allatu māṅkāyiñci. ūṟukāyākap payaṉpaṭuvatu; ayapēṭi - paipēnaca.
kūṭṭuc col:
iñcik kiḻaṅku pe. ginger-root.
iñcittēṟu, pe. iñcit tuṇṭu; small piece of green ginger.
iñcippāku, pe. iñci iḷakiya (lēkiya) vakai; a kind of ginger electuary.
iñcip pāvai, pe. iñcik kiḻaṅku (malaipaṭu. 125, urai). ginger, as bearing the shape of a doll.
iñci yūṟukāy = kāyac carakkuc cērttu eṇṇeyil ūṟa vaitta iñci, ginger-pickle
iñci vēr, pe. iñcik kiḻaṅku ginger - root.
iñcic 'curacam' eṉpatai iñcikkarukku eṉṟum, iñci 'murappā' eṉpatai iñci vaṭippu eṉṟum, collalām.
marapu vaḻakku : iñci tiṉṟa kuraṅkupōṟ pañcarittal(tontaravu ceytal).
toṭarmoḻi : iñci tiṉṟa kuraṅku.
uvamaip paḻamoḻi : iñci tiṉṟa kuraṅku pōla.
collamaivu :
iñci (iraṇṭiṟkum potu. 'iḷ' vēr; 'iñcu' mutaṉilai; 'i' viṉaimutalīṟu.
col varalāṟu:
iñci1 : ul - ol. ollutal = poruntutal. ul - ur - uṟu. uṟutal = poruntutal. ceṟital, valiyuṟutal. uṟu - uṟuti = tiṇṇam, valimai.
ur -uram = valimai uṟu - uṟa. uṟattal = ceṟital, iṟukutal.
"viṟappum uṟappum veṟuppum ceṟivē" (tol-col.348).
uṟanta viñci = iṟukiya matil.

aḷḷal = nerukkam. aḷḷākutal = ceṟital.
aḷḷiruḷ = ceṟinta iruṭṭu. aḷḷutal = ceṟital (cīvaka. 614).
uḷ - iḷ - (iy) - (iyñcu) - iñcu.

	puḷ - piḷ - piy - (piyñcu) - piñcu.
	koḷ - koy. toḷ - toy. poḷ - poy. 
iñcutal = ceṟital, iṟukutal, tiṇital.
iñcu - iñci = tiṇinta matil vakai.
perumpālum cempu puṉaintiyaṟṟiyatākavum ciṟupāṉmai araitta cāntiṭ ṭamaittatākavum, pakaivarāl eḷitākak tākka muṭiyāvāṟu tiṇṇitākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa matil, ēṉaivakai matil kaḷiṉum mika iṟukiyiruttal paṟṟi iñci yeṉappaṭṭatu.
iñci2 : 'ī' aṇmaiyaic cuṭṭumāṟu utaṭṭaip piṉṉukku iḻut tolikkum uyiroli. ī - īl - īr - īrttal = iḻuttal. īrtal = iḻut taṟuttal, pallāṟ kaṭittiḻuttu urittal, iḻuttal, aṟuttal.

iṉaccol:
(1)	tiraviṭam : malaiyāḷam - iñci, kuṭakam - iñci. kōttam - iñc, pirākirutam (pāli) - ciṅki, ciṅkivēra.
(2)	āriyam : (camaṟkirutam) - cruṅkavēra, parci (parji) - ciṅkivēra.
	ME. gingivere, OFr gengibre, LL gingiber. L. zingiber, Gk. zingiberis, Skt. srngavera. E. ginger,

ciṟappuk kuṟippu :
iñci toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻnāṭṭil viḷaikkappeṟṟuvaruvatu, 
"ceyyāp pāvai vaḷarntu kaviṉ muṟṟik 
kāyaṅ koṇṭaṉa"	(malaipaṭu. 125-6)
"iñcivī virāya paintār pūṭṭic 
cāntupuṟat teṟinta tacumputuḷaṅ kirukkai"
	(patiṟṟup. 42: 10-11)
"mañcaḷum iñciyum mayaṅkaril valayattuc
ceñcuḷaip palaviṉ pāṟpakai yuṟukkum"	
(cilap. 10 : 74 - 5)
eṉṉum paṇṭaiyilakkiyap pakutikaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum. tamiḻakatti liruntu paṇṭai nāḷilum mēṉāṭukaṭku ēṟṟumatiyāṉa carakkukaḷil iñciyum oṉṟākum. atu vērāyiruppatāl iñci vēreṉṟum, kiḻaṅkāyiruppatāl iñcik kiḻaṅkeṉṟum, pāvai pōṉṟiruppatāl iñcippāvai eṉṟum collappaṭum. iñcivēr eṉṉum collē mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ palvēṟu vaṭivil vaḻakkup peṟṟatu.
tiruttam
camaṟkiruta āriyar iñci yeṉṉum teṉcollai āriyac collākak kāṭṭa vēṇṭi, cruṅkavēra eṉattirittu māṉkompu pōṉṟatu eṉap poruṭ karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvārāyiṉar. itu ceyaṟkai yāṉatum poruttamaṟṟatum ākum eṉpatai, varalāṟu maṭṭumiṉṟic colvaṭivum teḷiyakkāṭṭum. vaṭamoḻiyil cruṅka eṉpatu kompu eṉṟumaṭṭum poruḷpaṭum. vēra eṉpatu uṭampaik kuṟikkum. iv viraṇṭum eṅṅaṉam iṇaintu iñciyaik kuṟikkum? iṟṟai yaṟiviyalkaḷait teḷiya vaṟinta mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarum, camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akaramutaliyilum, irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu akaramutaliyilum, ākkacuppōrṭuc 
ciṟṟakaramutaliyilum, tiraviṭac coṟpiṟappiyal akaramutaliyilum camaṟ kiruta āriyar kūṟṟaiyē taḻuviyiruppatu mikamika viyappiṟkiṭamāṉatē. av vakara mutalikaḷuḷḷum, irupatām nūṟṟāṇ ṭakaramutali iñci vēr eṉpatai malaiyā (malāy)c collākak kuṟittiruppatu iṉ ṉum viyappāṉatē. itaṟkut tamiḻar veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantēṟikaḷ eṉṟu tavaṟākak karutiyiruppatē aṭippaṭaiyākum. vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭamoḻiyākiya parciyil ciṅkivēra eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatāl maṭṭum, atu vaṭacollākiviṭātu. am moḻi ataip piṟkālattu vaḻakkēṟṟiyirukkalām.
4. pati
(1)	col :-pati.
(2)	colvakai :- (part of speech of word-class) - viṉai. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉaiyum (e. va.) ceyappaṭu poruḷ kuṉṟiyaviṉaiyum (e. .).
(3)	puṭaipeyarcci (conjugation) : 8āvatu vakai. patikiṟēṉ (ni. kā.) patintēṉ (i. è£.), pativēṉ, (e. kā.).
(4)	poruḷum āṭcimēṟkōḷum : (meanings and illustrative quotations.)
ceyaṟpeyar vaṭivu : (gerunidial form)
pati - tal = (ce. kuṉṟā vi.) 1. pativēṭṭil eḻututal, to register, enter in writing 2. peyarp paṭṭiyiṟ cērttal, to take on the roll.
(ce. kuṉṟiya vi.) 1. āḻtal, to sink in, as the foot or a wheel in mud; to enter, penetrate, as into a soft body. vaṇṭic cakkaram cēṟṟiṟ patikiṟatu. (ulaka vaḻakku). 2. tāḻntiruttal, to be low-lying. as land; to be depressed, sunk, hollow, worn away. nilam patinti rukkiṟatu. (u.va.) 3. taṅkutal, to settle, abide; to perch, roost, as a bird, "paticeṉṟu patintaṉaṉ"(taṇikaippu. piramaṉ.2). 4. nilaiyātal, to be permanent, as a post. eṉakku araṇmaṉaiyiṟ patinta vēlaiyillai. (nāñ. u. va.) 5. ūṉṟutal, to be absorbed, engrossed, involved, as the mind in any object. maṉam pāṭattiṟ patikiṟatā? (u. va.) 6. muttirai, eḻuttaccu mutaliyaṉa aḻuntutal, to be imprinted, impressed, marked, stamped, engraven, indented. 7. atikāram peṟutal, to be invested with power, authority. (W.). 8. iṟaṅkutal, to decline after meridinal transists; to be near setting, as a heavenly body; to descend, alight, as a bird. 9. vilai taṇital, to be low, as price. vilaiyaip patiyak kēṭṭāṉ. (u.va.) 10. amaitiyātal, to be mild, gentle or tractalle; to become submissive, modest or humble. paiyaṉ paḷḷikkūṭattiṟ patintirukkiṟāṉ. (u. va.) 11. añci yiṇaṅkutal allatu piṉvāṅkutal, to quail, flinch.
peyareccam : (adjectival participle)
i. kā. pe. patinta nilam, low land.
ceyaṟpeyarum toḻiṟpeyarum : (gerund and verbal noun.)
patital, patikai, pativatu, the act of registering, sinking etc.
pativu, registration, sinking etc.
toḻiṟpeyarum, toḻilākupeyarum : (verbal noun and metonymical verbal noun.) 
pati = 1. patikai, penetration, transfixion, thrust. "nuṇṇilaivēl patikoṇṭu," (cīvaka. 1186). 2. patiyam, cluster of saplings planted temporarily. 3. nāṟṟu, sapling for transplantation. 4. pativiḷakku, lamp fixed on a pot while exorcizing devils (W.). 5. à¬øõ¤ìñ¢, adobe, residence (î¤õ£.), "patiyiṟ kalaṅkiya mīṉ" (kuṟaḷ. 1116), 6. vīṭu, home, house (tivā.) 7. kōyil, temple (caṅ. aka.) 8. kuṟi collum iṭam. an oracular shrine, "patiyirunta pati yellām pativākac ceṉṟēṉ" (nāñ. marumak. mā.) 9. ūr, town, city, village. "patiyeḻu vaṟiyāp paḻaṅkuṭi" (cilap. 1:15) 10. ñālam (pūmi), the earth. (tailava. taiva.) 11. kutirai, horse. (aka. ni.)
pativu = 1. aḻuntukai, impression, indentation. 2. paḷḷam, lowness of a surface; depression. nilam pativāyirukkiṟatu. (u. va) 3. patiyam. cluster of saplings planted temporarily. 4. viṇmīṉkaḷiṉ cāyvu declination of a heavenly body. (W). 5. eḻuvataṟku muṉṉuḷḷa tiṅkaḷ (cantiraṉ) nilai, situation of the moon before rise. (W.) 6. patukkam, stooping, crouching, lurking as a thief of a beast ready to spring on its prey; ambush. 7. maṉam ūṉṟukai. engrossment, absorpion in an object or pursuit. 8. kaṇakkup patikai, registering, entering, as in account. 9. pativu ceyyappaṭṭatu. that which is registered. 10. patiyappaṭṭatu. registry, entry. 11. tīrmāṉikkappaṭṭa celavu, allotment, as in a budget. "intak kōvilil caṅku ūtuvataṟkup pativu ēṟpaṭa villai" (nāñ. u. va.) 12. nilaippu (nilaivaram), permanence. "intavūril avaṉ pativāyirukkiṟāṉ" (u. va.) 13. vaḻakkam, custom, habit. 14. amaiti, submission, obedience, humility. "pativāy naṭantukoḷ" (u.va.) 15. vilaittaṇivu. lowness of price (W.).
patiyam = 1. nāṟṟu muṭiyai iraṇṭoru nāṭkuc cēṟṟil patittu vaikkai, temporary planting of a cluster or saplings in mud. 2. ūṉṟi naṭuñ ceṭi koṭi kiḷai mutaliyaṉa, slip, shoot, graft. 3. ilaippāci, a species of duckweed. "ceṟiyaṇip patiyattiṭai vaḷariḷañ cēlkaḷ" (iraku. nāṭṭup.7).
patiyam - patikam = "patikam parikkuṅ kuḻal" (maṟaicai. 17.)

pātam); a fourth part of a duration of a naksatra. 8. toṇṭu, vaḻi pāṭu, ōkam, ōti eṉṉum nāṉku civacamaya neṟi (cariyāpātam, kiriyā pātam, yōkapātam, ñāṉapātam eṉṟa nāṉku caiva camaya mārkkam) - ce. pa. ka. ka. ta. a. (Madras university Tamil lexicon.)
pātak kāppu = pe. 1. ceruppu, slippers, sandals, clogs(w). 2. tiruvaṭip pātukāval; protection at the feet of a great person. "pātak kāppiṉaḷ paintoṭi yākaliṉ" (cilap. 14:23).
pātak kuṟaṭu = pe. kumiḻkoṇṭa kaṭṭai mitiyaṭi; knobbed wooden sandals.
pāta kaṭakam (pātak kaṭakam) = pe. pāṭakam, anklet (cūṭā.).
pāta kāṇikkai (pātak kāṇikkai) = pe. 1. kuruk koṭai; present to a spritual teacher laid at his feet. 2. cilavūrkaḷil puṉcey nilaṅkaḷukkākak kuṭikaḷāl kuṟunila maṉṉarukkuc celuttappaṭum oruvakai vari; a money payment made in certain villages by ryots of drylands to ther landlords.
pātac cāyai = pe. aṭiniḻal; human shadow.
pāta cakkaram (pātaccakkaram) = pe. pātattil varum oruvakaip puṇ; a kind of festering sore in the foot.
pāta cālakam (pātaccālakam) = pe. kālaṇi vakai; a foot ornament. "paranta mēkalaiyuṅ kōtta pātacālakamum" (kamparā, ayōt. kōlaṅ. 12).
pātacālakam - pātacālam (pātaccālam) = pe. kālaṇi vakai "pāta cālatta meṉkāl" (iraku. nāṭṭup. 48).
pāta tāmarai (pātat tāmarai) = pe. tiruvaṭit tāmarai; lotus - like feet.
pāta tūḷi (pātattūḷi) = pe. periyōr aṭippoṭi; dust of the feet of great persons "ēttuvārkaḷuḻakkiya pātatūḷi paṭutalāl" (tiv. periyāḻ. 4:4.6).
pāta pūcai (pātappūcai) = pe. kuru mutaliyōriṉ tiruvaṭi kaḷaik kaḻuvi malariṭṭu vaḻipaṭukai; worshipping the feet of a religious preceptor or revered person by washing and adorning them with flowers.
pāta mayakku = pe. 1. aṭi mayakku; aṭi muṟai māṟṟakkūṭiya ceyyuḷ; stanza whose lines are capable of transposition. 2. vēṟu pulavar pāṭiya mūvaṭikaḷōṭu tāṉ ōraṭi pāṭi muṭikkum miṟaippā vakai; a kind of artificial stanza of four lines the first three of which are taken from works of other poets while the last is composed by the author. (yāp. vi. 96. pak 504.).
pāta muttirai = pe āciriyaṉ tiruvaṭic cuvaṭu; imprint of a guru's feet (W.).
pāta mūlam = pe. 1. kutikāl heel, (yāḻ aka.) 2. vīṭu pēṟ ṟiṟkuk karaṇiyamāṉatum (kāraṇamāṉatum) aṭaikkalamākak karutappaṭu vatumāṉa tiruvaṭi; feet of a deity or saint considered as the source of bliss and a refuge. "niṉaiyumiṉ piṇṭināta ṉalaṅkiḷar pātamūlam" (cīvaka. 511).
pāta veṭippu = pe. pitta veṭippu; fissure-foot
pātam - pātai = pe. 1. oṟṟaiyaṭi vaḻi; foot-path. beaten track. 2. vaḻi, way, path, road (pi.ṅ.). 3. muṟai way, method, mode, manner.
pati - vati, ce. kuṉṟiya vi.

vati - tal = (ce. kuṉṟiya vi.) 1. taṅkutal, kuṭiyiruttal, to slay, to dwell, abide; to sojourn. "vatimaṇ vampalar vāyaviḻn taṉṉār" (paripā. 10:20). 2. tuyilutal, to sleep "āṟṟā ṇiṉaiyunaḷ vatintak kāl" (kalit. 126).
vati = pe. 1. vilaṅku paṟavai mutaliyaṉa taṅkumiṭam; lair, nest. "māvati cēra" (kalit. 119). 2. kāl patiyuñ cēṟu, mire. "ceṅkayal vatikkuti koḷum puṉalatu" (tēvā. 413:7), (piṅ).

iṉac col : (cognates and allied words)
1. tiraviṭam:
malaiyāḷam - pati, pativu.
patikka, to impress. patiyuka, to be impressed, be pressed down. pati, being fixed in, pressed down. pativu, settlement, custom.
patam, softness, elasticity, yielding temper. patama, patuma. pliancy. patarmma, rottenness of rice through damp, softness of mind. patukka, to be soft, tender, pliable. patuppu, softness. patuppikka, to soften patam, the right degree of ripeness, temperature, etc.
teluṅku - pataṉu, patuṉu, moisture, dampness, wetness; ripeness maturity temper.
kaṉṉaṭam - pata, proper or good state or condition, proper degree or temperature, the seasoning of any food, the right degree of a ripeness, keenness of edge or sharpness hata, proper condition
tuḷuvam - pataṉuṉi, pataṇuṉi, to become soft.
kuṭakam - pata soft.
kōttam - patm, temper of iron
mālattam - petke, to be soft. patō, sharp.
kuvī - pete. soft and damp.
pirākuvī - putēṉ, cold, cool, putī, coldness, frost.

ciṟappuk kuṟippu:
pata, pāta eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkuva tāl, tamiḻp pulavar palar avaṟṟai vaṭacolleṉṟē mayaṅki aṭi eṉṉum collaip pātam eṉṉum poruḷil āṇṭuvarukiṉṟaṉar. aṭi vēṟu; pātam vēṟu. aṭi eṉpatu oru poruḷiṉ aṭippakuti yaiyum ōr uyiriyiṉ kālaiyum pātattaiyum kuṟikkum potuc col. e-ṭu: aṭittaṭṭu, mayilaṭi, tiruvaṭi, aṭip pakuti yeṉpatu kīḻ iṭattaiyum kīḻp poruḷaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. e-ṭu: pantalaṭi, tēraṭi, ciṟiya tiruvaṭi periya tiruvaṭi. maravaṭi eṉpatu kālpōṉṟa aṭimaram. pātam eṉpatō nilattiṟ patiyum paranta aṭiyuṟuppaiyē allatu pakutiyaiyē kuṟikkum ciṟappuc col. e-ṭu: pātat tāmarai, pātam vaitta viḷakku. ākavē, patital eṉpatai vērp poruḷākak koṇṭa patam, pātam eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum tūya teṉ collē yeṉṟum, avaiyē muṟaiyē pata, pāta eṉa vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum eṉṟum aṟika. iṉi, pātam paṭuvatāl ēṟpaṭum vaḻiyaik kuṟikkum pātai yeṉṉum col, āṅkilattil (path eṉa) iruppataiyum vaṭamoḻiyil iṉmaiyaiyum, nōkkuka.
vas (vaci), eṉṉum camaṟkiruta viṉaic coṟkum. ataṉ tirivāṉa 'was' eṉṉum āṅkila iṟanta kāla viṉaiccoṟkum, vati eṉṉum 
tamiḻccol mūlamāyiruppatiṉiṉṟu, tamiḻiṉ toṉmaiyaiyum muṉ maiyaiyum aṟika.

(4) poruḷum āṭci mēṟkōḷum : (meanings and illustrative quotations)
ceyaṟpeyar vaṭivu : (Gerundial form)
poṟu-ttal, (ªê.kuù¢ø£ vi.) 1. cumattal, to bear, sustain. "civikai poṟuttāṉō ṭūrntā ṉiṭai" (kuṟaḷ. 37). "icaiyilā yākkai poṟutta nilam" (kuṟaḷ. 239). 2. tāṅkutal. to prop, to support, "ālattu... neṭuñ ciṉai vīḻpoṟuttāṅku" (puṟanā. 58). 3. aṇital, to put on. "ammaṇi poṟuttireṉ ṟaṟaintāṉ" (upatēcakā. uruttirāk. 44). 4. uṭaṉ koṇ ṭiruttal, to continue, to possess, as one's own body. "poṟukki lēṉuṭal pōkkiṭaṅ kāṇēṉ" (tiruvāca. 23:6). 5. tuṉpam poṟuttal. to endure, suffer. 6. kuṟṟam poṟuttal, to bear with to put up with. 7. iḷakkāraṅ koṭuttal, to indulge to allow. 8. maṉṉittal, to excuse, forgive, pardon. "tammai yikaḻvārp poṟuttal talai" (kuṟaḷ. 151). 9. piṟa karuttu vēṟupāṭṭai allatu mata vēṟupāṭṭaip poṟuttuk koḷḷutal, to tolerate. 10. poṟuppu (uttaravātam) ēṟṟal, to take responsibility to be accountable for. 11. kālan tāḻttal, to delay, to postpone. 12. uvamaiyākap peṟutal, to be similar. "ulakam poṟukkāta tōḷāy" (cīvaka. 402). 13. aḻuttutal, to press heavily, as a load.
(ªê.kuù¢ø¤ò vi.) 1. poṟumaiyāyiruttal, to be patient, exercise forbearance. 2.viṉaiyiṭai niṉṟu koḷḷutal. to stop, wait halt in doing anything. 3.vilai koḷḷutal, to cost, as an article; to be spent or expended on. 4. tōṇi taṭṭippōtal, to run aground, as a vessel, to strand. 5. māṭṭik 
koḷḷutal, to become fixed, jammed, wedged in (W.) 6. kaṭamaiyākak cumarutal, to devolve upon, as a duty resposibility expenses. 7. iṇaṅki yiruttal, to consent yield, comply with 8. mikuntiruttal, to be excessive. 9. kaṉattal, to be heavy, weight.

caṟṟē poṟu, wait a little.
